- **outlining**: Use when asked to outline a document, lay out its structure or pull its key points; to inventory the claims a text makes, presupposed ones included, before editing or fact-checking it; to read a reply, document or transcript as the questions it answers, for a question margin or to see what a conversation settled and left open; or to judge whether a document is too detailed or too thin. Settles the unit, grain and dimension, then produces entries tied to the source and checks their coverage. ([mehrlander/web-tools](https://github.com/mehrlander/web-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/outlining) · ★2)
- **outlining**: Use when starting a new paper, when `.writing/outline.md` is missing or needs a major restructure, or when someone asks for a section's prose while the structure is still unsettled ("论文一个字都没有", "大纲还没定", "先从 Evaluation 动笔"). That last request routes here, not to drafting. Converts a research idea into an IMRAD outline plus a per-section claim list through an interleaved interview, producing `.writing/outline.md`, `.writing/claims/section_*.md`, and a filled `.writing/metadata.yaml`. No manuscript prose is written here, this is the spec phase before drafting. ([SipengXie2024/superpower-writing](https://github.com/SipengXie2024/superpower-writing/tree/HEAD/skills/outlining) · ★2)
